### [103] [Predicting Brain Morphometry with MT-GNN: Mesh Evolution in Continuous Time with Graph-Based Metric Tensor Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05132)
*Hao Ding,Daniel Semchin,Paul M. Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-GNN模型，通过预测皮层下结构表面的内在几何度量（第一基本形式）并结合可微的ARAP求解器端到端解码为网格表面，实现对任意时间跨度的纵向形状演化预测。在ADNI数据集的14个皮层下结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测时域上均优于现有方法（平均顶点误差降低2.29%，14/14结构均胜出）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量先验扫描预测皮层下结构形状的演化对于预后判断和临床试验富集具有重要意义。现有方法要么通过高维嵌入外推形状轨迹，要么直接回归顶点形变，缺乏对表面内在几何结构的显式建模，且难以处理任意访问历史和预测时域。

Method: 提出单图网络结构的MT-GNN：对每个皮层下结构，图网络以任意因果多访问历史为输入，以傅里叶编码的条件时域为条件，预测逐顶点的第一基本形式（度量张量）。预测的度量通过可微的As-Rigid-As-Possible求解器解码为表面网格，模型在刚性对齐顶点误差上端到端训练。

Result: 在ADNI数据集的14个皮层下结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测时域上表现最佳：平均顶点误差比时间均值降低2.29%（p=6.1×10^-5），14/14个结构均优于基线；相比DCM（-0.19%）和TransforMesh（-0.45%，p=1.2×10^-4）均有显著提升，且随着预测时域增长优势扩大。

Conclusion: 预测表面内在几何而非直接回归顶点形变，结合可微重构进行端到端训练，能够保证解码表面有效且持续改善预测精度，为纵向网格形状演化预测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Predicting how a subcortical structure's shape will evolve from a few prior scans could support prognosis and clinical-trial enrichment. Existing longitudinal mesh predictors either extrapolate shape trajectories via high-dimensional embeddings or regress vertex deformations directly. We instead predict the surface's intrinsic geometry in continuous time: a single per-structure graph network predicts the future per-vertex first fundamental form (metric tensor) for an arbitrary causal multiple-visit history and an arbitrary prediction horizon, conditioned on a Fourier encoding of the lead time. The predicted metric is decoded into a surface by a differentiable As-Rigid-As-Possible solver, and the model is trained end-to-end on the rigid-aligned vertex error. Training through the reconstruction keeps the decoded prediction a valid surface and consistently improves it. On 14 subcortical structures from the ADNI dataset, the proposed mesh evolution model (MT-GNN) predicts best among the evaluated methods at every horizon ($-2.29\%$ mean vertex error vs. the temporal mean, $p{=}6.1{\times}10^{-5}$, beating it on 14/14 structures), ahead of geodesic shape regression (DCM, $-0.19\%$) and a mesh transformer (TransforMesh, $-0.45\%$; $p{=}1.2{\times}10^{-4}$), with the lead widening as the horizon grows.

</details>


### [104] [HelloWorld: Enabling Socially Interactive Characters in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05070)
*Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Xuangeng Chu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelloWorld是一个支持用户与视频世界中角色进行社交互动的视频世界模型，用户按下按钮即可提示屏幕角色对镜头做出回应（如转身、挥手、点头或简短问候）。该模型通过自蒸馏pipeline微调视频生成模型，并引入无需训练的模块来控制交互发生的时间窗口，同时构建了HelloWorldBench基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频世界模型近期取得了显著进展，但用户与视频世界中角色之间的社交互动仍然不被支持，这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 1) 提出自蒸馏pipeline：在模型自身合成的数据上微调视频生成模型，每个合成片段同时包含社交互动和相机运动，使模型学习相机姿态条件而不降低交互质量；2) 推理阶段引入无需训练的模块：通过调制DiT的交叉注意力掩码，使交互相关的文本提示仅关注按下按钮的时间窗口内的帧，从而在时间上定位角色的响应。

Result: HelloWorld在交互质量上超越了多种基线方法，同时在图片美学和相机姿态跟随方面保持了最先进的水平。构建的HelloWorldBench包含400个样本，含3个社交交互指标和3个传统指标。

Conclusion: HelloWorld成功实现了视频世界模型中的社交互动功能，且不牺牲生成质量，为该领域开辟了新的交互范式。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable recent progress of video world models, social interaction between users and the characters within these worlds remains unsupported. To fill this gap, we present HelloWorld, a video world model that enables social interaction with in-world characters. With a single button press, users can prompt the on-screen character to respond toward the camera, e.g., turning to the viewer, waving, nodding, or speaking a short greeting. To make these interactions natural, we propose a self-distillation pipeline that finetunes the video generation model on data synthesized by itself. Each synthesized clip contains both social interactions and camera motion, allowing the model to learn camera-pose conditioning without degrading interaction quality. At inference, we further introduce a training-free module that determines when the interaction occurs. Upon a button press, it modulates the cross-attention masks of the DiT so that the interaction-related text prompt attends only to the frames within the press window, temporally localizing the character's response. We further build HelloWorldBench, a 400-sample benchmark with three social interaction metrics alongside three conventional metrics, for evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that HelloWorld surpasses a variety of baselines in interaction quality, while maintaining state-of-the-art picture aesthetics and camera-pose following. Project page: https://github.com/AlayaLab/HelloWorld

</details>


### [105] [Bag-of-Visual-Words for Spatial Mapping of Lung Adenocarcinoma Growth Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05074)
*Darya Ardan,Valentin Oreiller,Henning Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉词袋（BoVW）的弱监督管道，用于在整张切片图像（WSI）上对肺腺癌（LUAD）生长模式进行空间映射。该方法从少量标注的感兴趣区域（ROI）中学习视觉词汇，通过均值BoVW直方图构建模式原型，并使用Jensen-Shannon散度进行最近原型分类，最终在WSI瓦片网格上生成可解释的空间模式图。在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上的评估显示，肿瘤/健康分类达到0.974的平衡准确率，组织学分级分类在所有编码器上均优于监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LUAD生长模式空间映射方法在单个瓦片级别操作，仅产生通用形态学聚类而非临床定义的模式图，缺乏区域级别的架构上下文解析能力。

Method: 采用弱监督Bag-of-Visual-Words（BoVW）管道：从冻结的foundation model嵌入中提取特征，从少量标注ROI中学习视觉词汇；构建同标签ROI的均值BoVW直方图作为模式原型；使用Jensen-Shannon散度进行滑动窗口区域的最近原型分类；将预测投影到WSI瓦片网格上生成空间模式图。

Result: 在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上，使用三种foundation model编码器和多种词汇量进行两个临床任务评估。肿瘤/健康分类最佳配置（H-Optimus-1）达到0.974平衡准确率，接近监督SVM的0.987；组织学分级分类中BoVW管道在所有编码器上均优于监督基线。

Conclusion: BoVW管道能够生成与临床相关、可解释的LUAD空间模式图，且ROI级别的模式分解保留了对分级分类重要的异质性信息，优于全局均值池化的监督基线。

Abstract: Spatial mapping of lung adenocarcinoma (LUAD) growth patterns across whole slide images (WSIs) requires resolving architectural context at the region level, yet existing methods operate at the individual tile level and produce generic morphological clusters rather than clinically defined pattern maps. We propose a weakly supervised Bag-of-Visual-Words (BoVW) pipeline that learns a visual vocabulary from frozen foundation model embeddings extracted from a small set of annotated regions of interest (ROIs). Pattern prototypes are constructed as mean BoVW histograms of same-label ROIs and used for nearest-prototype classification of sliding-window regions under Jensen--Shannon divergence. The resulting predictions are projected onto the WSI tile grid to produce interpretable spatial pattern maps. We evaluate the method on 87 CPTAC-LUAD patients using three foundation model encoders and multiple vocabulary sizes on two clinically motivated tasks. For tumour/healthy classification, the best configuration achieves a balanced accuracy of $0.974$ with H-Optimus-1, approaching the $0.987$ obtained by a supervised SVM trained on mean-pooled WSI embeddings. For binary histologic grade classification, the BoVW pipeline achieves higher balanced accuracy than the supervised baseline for all encoders, suggesting that ROI-level pattern decomposition preserves grade-relevant heterogeneity that is attenuated by global mean pooling.

</details>


### [106] [Lesion Detection in CT with Frozen Self-Distilled Features: SALT, a Spatially Adaptive Label-Guided Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05100)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Evan W. Damron,Mitchell A. Klusty,Caroline N. Leach,Emily B. Collier,V. K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT方法，通过在自蒸馏预训练中基于弱标注（box标注）对编码器patch网格上的教师温度进行锐化并提高掩码patch损失权重，使病灶区域的训练信号增强，而无需改变模型输入或下游推理时的标注条件。在四个CT队列的3D病灶检测任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督预训练目标在空间上是均匀的：教师温度和每个patch的损失权重在图像各处完全相同，因此一个仅占几个patch的病灶对训练信号的贡献不超过周围正常组织。已有工作通过偏置视图朝向标注区域来改变模型所见内容，但并未在目标函数层面施加压力。

Method: SALT方法在自蒸馏预训练中，利用仅预训练阶段可用的弱box标注，在编码器patch网格上定义紧凑区域。在该区域内：1) 锐化教师softmax温度；2) 提高掩码patch损失权重。其余目标函数、掩码策略和centering统计均保持不变。编码器在下游使用时为纯特征提取器，无标签、无条件依赖。

Result: 通过冻结编码器并仅训练轻量级多深度CenterNet风格头部，在四个CT队列上检测3D病灶。报告了patch级可分性、按队列和病灶大小分层的3D检测结果、box质量，以及一个无需配准、掩码或微调的探测器自由探针（单个冻结patch嵌入在随访扫描中重新识别病灶）。对照实验隔离了机制，与架构、预训练数据、调度和标签引导裁剪完全相同但无目标条件化的backbone对比。

Conclusion: SALT通过空间指标而非标签语义表达条件化，因此可适配任何弱空间标注形式，且训练信号增强作用于目标层面而非输入层面，在下游保持纯特征提取器的简洁性，在病灶检测任务上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining objectives are spatially uniform: the teacher temperature and the per-patch loss weight are identical everywhere in the image, so a lesion a few patches wide contributes no more to the training signal than the surrounding parenchyma. Prior work biases the views toward annotated regions, which changes what the model sees but adds no pressure on the objective. We instead condition the targets of self-distillation, a method we call SALT (Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature). Weak, box-derived labels, available only during pretraining, define a compact region on the encoder's patch grid, inside which the teacher's softmax temperature is sharpened and the masked-patch loss is up-weighted. The objectives, the masking policy and the centering statistics are otherwise unchanged, and at every downstream use the encoder is a plain feature extractor with no labels and no conditioning. We evaluate by freezing the encoder and training only a lightweight multi-depth CenterNet-style head, detecting lesions in 3D on four CT cohorts, and we isolate the mechanism against a backbone identical in architecture, pretraining data, schedule and label-guided cropping but with no target conditioning. We report patch-level separability, 3D detection stratified by cohort and by lesion size, box quality, and a detector-free probe in which a single frozen patch embedding re-identifies a lesion in a follow-up scan without registration, masks or fine-tuning. Because the conditioning is expressed through a spatial indicator rather than through label semantics, the formulation admits any weak spatial annotation; we instantiate and validate it for lesions.

</details>


### [107] [HexMIL: Hierarchical Attention MIL for Ante-Hoc Explainable Detection of AI-Manipulated CT Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05101)
*Orazio Pontorno,Luca Guarnera,Zahid Akhtar,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HexMIL，一种基于层次可解释多实例学习的无掩码医学深度伪造检测器。它通过两级层次的patch和slice聚合，利用门控注意力机制在仅有体级别二进制标签监督下，同时实现跨生成架构的伪造检测和无需像素级标注的3D定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学深度伪造（由深度生成模型操纵的医学图像）严重威胁临床工作流程，而现有检测器存在两个关键缺陷：对未见生成架构的泛化能力差，以及缺乏可解释性。

Method: HexMIL将每个CT体分解为patch和slice两级层次结构，通过独立的Gated Attention模块进行聚合，注意力权重直接组合成全分辨率3D注意力体，实现篡改子区域的定位。注意力权重构成分类决策的前向计算本身（ante-hoc），而非Grad-CAM等事后方法。

Result: 在M3DSynth和CT-GAN数据集上采用跨生成器泛化协议（单架构训练、未见架构测试），HexMIL在域外分类中比所有基线高出+9.1 AUC和+9.4 F1，并在定位任务中取得最佳平均IoU和Pointing Game分数。

Conclusion: HexMIL通过层次注意力结构同时解决了医学深度伪造检测的泛化性和可解释性问题，在跨生成器场景下显著优于现有方法，且无需像素级标注即可提供结构忠实的前瞻性空间归因。

Abstract: The emergence of medical deepfakes, i.e., medical images manipulated by deep generative models, poses a significant threat to clinical workflows. However, existing detectors suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to unseen generative architectures for manipulation detection and lack of interpretability. In this context, we present HexMIL (Hierarchical EXplainable Multiple Instance Learning), a mask-free medical deepfake detector that simultaneously addresses both limitations using only binary volume-level supervision. HexMIL decomposes each CT volume into a two-level hierarchy of patches and slices, aggregated via independent Gated Attention modules whose weights are directly combined into a full-resolution 3D attention volume that localizes the manipulated sub-region without any pixel-level annotation. Unlike post-hoc methods such as Grad-CAM, HexMIL's attention weights constitute the exact forward computation driving the classification decision, providing ante-hoc and structurally faithful spatial attribution. We evaluate HexMIL on M3DSynth and CT-GAN datasets under a rigorous cross-generator generalization protocol, training on a single generative architecture and testing on unseen ones. HexMIL outperforms all baselines by $+9.1$ AUC and $+9.4$ F1 in out-of-domain classification, and achieves the best average IoU and Pointing Game score in localization. Project page: opontorno.github.io/hexmil.

</details>


### [108] [IRIS: A Visual Cortex-Inspired Framework for Analyzing Orientation Selectivity in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05122)
*Vaishnavi B Mohan,Vijayakrishna Naganoor,Yashas Annadani,Shashank Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉Transformer（ViT）中方向选择性这一生物启发性特征如何涌现，提出了一套神经科学启发的度量指标，发现训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，并提供了如何选择微调层数的机制性启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT已成为许多感知任务的标准图像编码器，但由于其缺乏归纳偏置（全局而非局部信息处理），其编码低级特征的机制尚不清楚。生物视觉系统通过局部感受野构建方向选择性等低级特征，且这些特征是跨神经通路共享的通用表征。作者希望探究这类生物基础特征是否在ViT中涌现。

Method: 引入一套神经科学启发的度量指标来量化ViT中的方向选择性编码：(1) 表征相似性得分(RSS)用于衡量表征几何中的方向编码；(2) 方向招募得分(ORS)用于量化方向选择性单元的数量；(3) 方向调谐带宽。通过这些指标，从模型深度、训练过程、模型规模等多个维度系统分析方向选择性在ViT中的涌现机制。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，共享目标的模型无论规模大小都在相对深度相似的位置达到峰值；(2) 许多单元在训练早期就具有方向选择性，早期到中间的层在训练过程中会招募更多此类单元，而深层单元则失去选择性并拓宽调谐朝向语义编码；(3) 这些度量指标为选择最佳解冻层数以获得下游泛化性能提供了机制性启发。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在ViT训练过程中追踪生物基础特征的方法，能够探测目标属性如何在Transformer表征中被编码，并建立了对ViT跨任务泛化能力的系统性理解。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have become the de facto standard for image encoding across many perception tasks. Despite their empirical success, it remains mechanistically unclear how they encode low-level features, given their lack of inductive biases: ViTs process information globally rather than relying on local structure. Biological visual systems, in contrast, build low-level features, such as orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex, by combining information from small, localized regions of the visual field. These features are general-purpose representations, shared and required across multiple specialized neural pathways, unlike higher-level, task-specific semantic features. This raises the question if such biologically-grounded features arise in ViTs. In this work, we systematically study how orientation selectivity emerges in ViTs by introducing a suite of neuroscience-inspired metrics: representational similarity score (RSS), orientation recruitment score (ORS), and orientation tuning bandwidth to quantify how orientation is encoded in representational geometry and as a function of model depth. Through extensive analysis, we find that: (1) the training paradigm is the strongest determinant of orientation selectivity, with models sharing an objective, peaking at comparable relative depths regardless of scale (2) many units are orientation-selective early in training, with early-to-middle layers recruiting more such units over time, while deeper layers lose selectivity and broaden their tuning toward semantic encoding and (3) our metrics offer a mechanistic heuristic for how many layers to unfreeze for best downstream generalization. Our framework presents a way to track biologically-grounded features during ViT training, probes how desired properties are encoded in transformer representations, and builds a systematic understanding of how ViTs generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [109] [SmartMage: Dynamic Modality Orchestration for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05137)
*Yue Zhang,Yingzhao Jian,Yunqiu Xu,Xiaoxiao Sun,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartMage，一个统一的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），通过语义引导的模态自适应路由（SMART模块）和模态感知门控专家（MAGE模块），动态编排异构模态以进行语义感知的3D场景理解，在五个3D场景理解基准和RGB-only视频理解基准上均取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型通常依赖固定的模态组合，忽略了查询相关的模态需求，导致无关模态引入语义噪声、信息丰富的模态利用不足，造成计算浪费和推理稀释。3D场景理解需要联合推理视觉和几何等多种异构模态，亟需一种能根据查询语义动态选择模态的方法。

Method: SmartMage包含两个核心模块：(1) Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART)模块，利用语义先验、文本-模态对齐和模态质量来动态选择任务相关的模态；(2) Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE)模块，利用模态先验引导专家激活，促进多模态推理中的自适应专业化。

Result: SmartMage在五个3D场景理解基准上达到最先进性能，在RGB-only视频理解基准上也取得了有竞争力的结果。在诊断基准ScanFacet上，任务被划分为细粒度语义类别，观察到的模态-语义模式进一步证明了SmartMage的有效性。

Conclusion: SmartMage通过动态模态编排有效解决了固定模态组合的局限性，在不同语义类型的查询中自适应选择最相关的模态，在3D场景理解任务中展现了优越性能，并提供了模态-语义模式的深入分析。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes is fundamental to embodied intelligence, requiring joint reasoning over heterogeneous information from multiple modalities, including visual and geometric cues. However, the relevance of these modalities often varies across queries. Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically rely on fixed modality combinations, overlooking query-dependent modality needs. Such a rigid design can introduce semantic noise from irrelevant modalities while underutilizing more informative ones, leading to wasted computation and diluted reasoning. To address these challenges, this paper proposes SmartMage, a unified MLLM that dynamically orchestrates heterogeneous modalities for semantic-aware 3D scene understanding. Specifically, SmartMage incorporates: (1) a Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART) module that selects task-relevant modalities using semantic priors, text-modality alignment, and modality quality; and (2) a Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE) module that leverages modality priors to guide expert activation, fostering adaptive specialization in multimodal reasoning. Empirically, SmartMage achieves state-of-the-art performance across five 3D scene understanding benchmarks, and attains competitive results on RGB-only video understanding benchmarks. In our diagnostic benchmark ScanFacet, tasks are divided into fine-grained semantic categories, enabling analysis of modality combinations preferred by each semantic type. The observed modality-semantic patterns provide further evidence of SmartMage's effectiveness. Project page: https://yuecheong.github.io/SmartMage/.

</details>


### [110] [Objects as Audio-Visual Modal Sound Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05145)
*Zisen Shao,Zihao Wei,Derong Jin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-MSF，一种结合多视角图像和少量冲击声录音的物体级声学表示方法，利用3D高斯溅射与稠密3D视觉特征构建几何先验，并用紧凑的物理模态参数表示冲击声场，实现少样本重建和高质量冲击声渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建主要关注物体几何和外观，忽略了通过物理交互产生的丰富声学线索；已有的冲击声建模方法要么依赖昂贵的物理仿真，要么需要大规模数据集进行纯数据驱动学习，缺乏高效且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Method: AV-MSF基于3D高斯溅射（3D Gaussian Splatting）构建，集成稠密3D视觉特征作为几何感知先验，并使用紧凑且物理意义明确的模态参数来表示冲击声场，实现少样本（few-shot）冲击声重建。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AV-MSF在冲击声渲染方面达到最先进水平，优于基于物理仿真和数据驱动的基线方法；此外还展示了接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。

Conclusion: AV-MSF成功将视觉与声学信息融合，以少样本方式实现高质量的物体级冲击声建模，验证了物理模态参数在声场表示中的有效性，并展示了其在接触定位和声音编辑等应用中的潜力。

Abstract: While modern 3D reconstruction excels at modeling object geometry and appearance, it largely ignores the rich acoustic cues revealed through physical interaction. Object impact sounds convey material, stiffness, and structural properties that complement vision, yet existing impact sound modeling approaches either rely on expensive physics-based simulation or require large datasets to generalize in a purely data-driven manner. We introduce Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF), a novel object-level acoustic representation reconstructed from multi-view images and only a few impact sound recordings. AV-MSF builds on 3D Gaussian Splatting integrated with dense 3D visual feature to provide a strong geometry-aware prior, and represents the impact sound field using compact, physically meaningful modal parameters, enabling robust few-shot reconstruction. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that AV-MSF achieves state-of-the-art impact sound rendering, outperforming both physics-based and data-driven baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate downstream applications enabled by our representation, including contact localization and object sound editing.

</details>


### [111] [CoCo-IR: Contextual Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05149)
*Shengcao Cao,Tanmaya Shekhar Dabral,Zhongli Ding,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Kaifeng Chen,Zhe Li,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的上下文组合图像检索任务（CoCo-IR），允许用户通过多轮对话逐步优化检索结果。作者基于大型多模态模型构建了一个上下文感知推理器，生成跨轮次演化的可变形图像嵌入（TIE），并设计了一个完全自主、可扩展的数据引擎来生成高质量训练数据。在单轮基准CIRCO上达到39.4 mAP@5，在多轮基准上达到44.1 R@1（4轮对话），显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于指令的图像检索系统仅限于单轮交互，无法捕捉现实生活中复杂视觉搜索的迭代特性。用户往往需要通过多轮对话逐步细化和调整检索条件，这是一个现有方法未能解决的重要缺陷。

Method: 1）提出CoCo-IR新任务，支持用户通过多轮交互渐进式优化检索；2）基于大型多模态模型构建上下文感知推理器，利用完整交互历史生成跨轮次演化的可变形图像嵌入（TIE）；3）开发全自动可扩展的数据引擎，利用LMM生成高质量上下文检索数据，并通过模型引导验证挖掘困难负样本。

Result: 在单轮基准CIRCO上达到39.4 mAP@5的新SOTA；在CoCo-IR基准的4轮对话上达到44.1 R@1，大幅超越现有方法（28.2 R@1）。

Conclusion: 提出的CoCo-IR框架有效解决了多轮上下文图像检索问题，通过LMM驱动的上下文推理和自主数据引擎，在单轮和多轮场景下均取得了显著优于现有方法的性能，为图像检索领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Current instruction-based image retrieval systems are powerful but limited to single-turn interactions, failing to capture the iterative nature of complex, real-world visual searches. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contextual Composed Image Retrieval (CoCo-IR), a novel task that enables users to progressively refine search results through interactions. We address this new task by proposing a new model based on a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) that functions as a context-aware reasoner for CoCo-IR. Our model interprets the entire interaction history to generate Transformable Image Embeddings (TIE) that evolve across turns. To fuel the model training without expensive human annotations, we develop a fully autonomous, scalable data engine that leverages LMMs to generate high-quality contextual retrieval data, and uses model-guided verification to mine challenging hard negatives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance: We achieve 39.4 mAP@5 on the challenging single-turn benchmark CIRCO; furthermore, on our new CoCo-IR benchmark, our model maintains robust performance with 44.1 R@1 on 4-turn dialogues, dramatically outperforming existing methods (28.2 4-turn R@1) that fail to handle multi-turn context. Project page: https://CoCo-IR.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C²MOE，一个基于一致性和互补性引导的混合专家框架，用于不完整多模态情感识别。该框架通过信息理论将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性两部分，并采用双分支预测机制和可学习重加权模块，在多种缺失模态场景下超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别在实际应用中常面临模态缺失问题，现有方法虽通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽视了模态间的互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差，模型性能严重下降。

Method: C²MOE框架包含三个核心部分：1) 通过交互感知专家将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性成分，一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获，互补性通过最大化条件熵保留；2) 双分支预测机制，一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态独特线索；3) 可学习重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性分数，实现鲁棒自适应的融合与插补。

Result: 在多个多模态情感识别基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C²MOE在各种缺失模态设置下一致性地超越了现有最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 同时考虑模态一致性和互补性对于不完整多模态情感学习至关重要，C²MOE通过统一的信息理论框架有效解决了模态缺失下的重建偏差问题，为不完整多模态学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [113] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对次优势（min-max）超度量算子（等价于单链接聚类的超度量诱导），提出了一个ℓ₀类型的稳定性理论。核心发现是稀疏编辑仅通过最小生成树（MST）传播，并给出了每个编辑的暴露割分数及树整体包络，证明了Hamming-Lipschitz界和最坏情况下的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的超度量稳定性理论通常基于ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量，这些界不适合稀疏扰动（仅改变少数成对距离）的场景，因为此类扰动可能远小于ℓ∞扰动但产生显著的结构变化。因此需要一种更精细的、基于稀疏编辑的稳定性分析框架。

Method: 作者建立了ℓ₀型稳定性理论，分析了稀疏编辑如何通过最小生成树传播：
1) 证明成对超度量值仅在树路径跨过被编辑的边或由编辑的树外边新暴露的割时才会改变；
2) 提出每个编辑的暴露割分数（exposed-cut score）和树整体包络；
3) 推导Hamming-Lipschitz界以限制可改变的超度量条目数；
4) 证明紧致性结果（树边界的精确达到和Θ(n²)改变量的显式族）；
5) 提出多编辑条件下的条件近可加性原则。

Result: 在严格割分离条件下树边界的界可精确达到；离线编辑存在显式族使一个编辑距离改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目；多编辑在认证的大改变区域和可忽略的聚合重叠条件下满足条件近可加性；在深度嵌入图上的实验表明结构分数可为层次表示提供有用的脆弱性诊断。

Conclusion: 稀疏扰动对超度量算子的影响完全由树几何决定，基于MST的暴露割分数能够精确刻画编辑的传播范围。这种ℓ₀稳定性理论弥补了传统ℓ∞/Gromov-Hausdorff框架在稀疏场景下的不足，并为层次表示提供了有效的脆弱性诊断工具。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [114] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个信任域框架来统一理解Adam等自适应矩估计优化方法的行为，推导出基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制族（Gmake），其中p=4时涉及峰度估计。实验表明，当信任域约束较弱时四阶矩版本优势最大，而较强约束下二阶矩版本更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望从理论角度解释Adam等自适应矩估计方法的工作机制，目前缺乏统一的框架来理解这些方法中归一化、学习率调度、动量谱低通滤波和算子级谱归一化等多样组件之间的内在联系。

Method: 提出信任域框架，将每个权重的更新步长限制在由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）支配的信任域内。由此推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制，p=4时涉及峰度估计，统称为Gmake，统一了多种归一化机制的解释。

Result: 通过在GPT2-124M模型上使用FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集的实验发现：当信任域约束较弱时，四阶矩（峰度）版本受益最大；当信任域约束逐步增强时，二阶矩版本越来越有竞争力，通常能达到比四阶矩版本略低甚至更低的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信任域框架成功统一解释了Adam等自适应优化机制中的多种组件，并揭示了不同阶矩估计在不同信任域约束强度下的适用性权衡：弱约束下高阶矩更优，强约束下二阶矩更佳。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [115] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索将机器学习框架与传统的R-Matrix代码相结合，用于自动检测中子透射光谱中的中子共振。使用全卷积神经网络对七个透射光谱中的点进行分类，虽然达到了93%的分类准确率，但该指标高估了模型的泛化能力，模型无法可靠地泛化到未见过的同位素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的中子透射数据分析依赖R-Matrix代码，这些代码需要大量人工操作且依赖先验评估结果，容易引入偏差。中子透射数据复杂且嘈杂，传统峰值识别方法难以处理，因此需要自动化方法来加速数据处理并减少人为偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对七个中子透射光谱（两个评估光谱和五个实验光谱）中的每个点进行分类，将其归类为共振区域或非共振区域。该方法旨在作为传统R-Matrix拟合代码的辅助工具。

Result: 模型在透射光谱分类中达到了约93%的准确率，但进一步分析表明该指标高估了模型的泛化能力。即使增加了训练数据（基于PHYSOR 2026的先前分析扩展），该方法仍无法可靠地泛化到此前未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 当前方法在泛化方面存在局限性，未来工作需要构建更大、更多样化的训练数据集以训练出具有更好泛化能力的模型，并将中子共振的已知物理特征纳入模型以提升性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [116] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Lindblad启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络储备池，将旋转与衰减作为独立设计变量，分别控制相位混合与记忆遗忘，同时通过衰减谱直接保证回声状态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储备池将信号混合、记忆保持和稳定性耦合在单一随机循环矩阵中，现有结构化设计（拓扑、范数保持、泄漏、深度）不能同时提供可逆混合与不可逆遗忘的独立模态控制以及直接的全局稳定性保证。

Method: 将储备池循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成，旋转与衰减成为独立设计变量；通过正交模态混合保持正规性，衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性裕度，无需事后谱半径缩放。

Result: 在十个对齐种子上与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环、下一代储备池及紧凑门控循环单元对比，在受限NARMA-20上取得最佳固定储备池性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，在最强线性记忆任务上持平，整体在广泛基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件性；该框架提供了混合、记忆和稳定性显式且可独立调节的可解释循环架构。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [117] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在开源3W数据集上，于现有开放世界学习（OWL）油井异常检测管线下游部署了基于Qwen3.5-397B-A17B混合专家模型的LLM代理层，提供自然语言解释、置信度排序的批评意见和异常簇命名。在989个真实井段上的实验显示分类准确率35.1% top-1/63.9% top-3，新颖性检测89.7%，旨在弥合OWL管线在运营场景中的可解释性鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL管线虽能回答"发生了什么"，但无法解释"模型为何如此判断"或"操作员下一步该做什么"，且无法为发现的新颖性聚类赋予人类可读的名称，导致其难以在实际运营场景中部署。本文旨在通过引入LLM代理层来弥补这一可解释性缺口。

Method: 在现有OWL管线（自编码器检测+多类分类+马氏距离新颖性检测）下游添加LLM代理层，使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B混合专家模型（经NVIDIA NIM服务），输入结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言论证、置信度排序的批评意见以及整合后的新颖性命名。代理作为上游方法的"伴侣"而非替代品，承担四项职责：确认上游决策、以传感器为依据的论证、标注分歧以及为新奇事件命名。

Result: 在989个真实3W数据集井段上的三项研究中，代理在全部九个类别上达到35.1% top-1/63.9% top-3分类准确率（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；7个探测类别上top-2验证准确率71.7% [64.8, 77.6]，精确率0.91 [0.84, 0.95]；新颖性检测准确率89.7% [87.0, 91.9]，5/7个隐藏类别获得稳定聚类命名。

Conclusion: LLM代理并非独立分类器，而是上游OWL方法的辅助组件，通过确认决策、提供可审计的传感器依据论证、标记不合理标注以及为新颖事件提供整合的可读标签，有效弥合了OWL管线在运营环境部署中的可解释性鸿沟。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [118] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tactus，一个仅依靠电阻式压力阵列数据回答文本查询的开放模型。在STAG基准上达到0.771±0.062 top-1准确率，匹配甚至超越有监督闭集CNN（0.76），且无需训练分类头。核心配方是小数据（187条训练记录）、掩码自编码器预训练（144k帧）以及传感器自身校准仿射变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最便宜、出货量最大的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表征学习研究几乎都集中在光学传感器（凝胶变形成像）上。作者希望填补这一空白，证明低成本压力阵列数据也能支撑强大的触觉语义理解。

Method: 采用掩码自编码器（MAE）在144k无标签同传感器帧上预训练，仅用187条训练记录，结合传感器标定仿射变换，无需训练分类头即可直接回答文本查询，模型开放发布。

Result: STAG基准（27对象）上top-1达0.771±0.062（四次运行），top-3达0.935，超越有监督CNN的0.76。误差集中在少数接触模糊类别，与文本目标几何无关（Spearman rho≤0.05），对改写和裸名称查询鲁棒，两帧即可恢复八帧89%准确率。同时报告了失败案例：跨传感器预训练无增益、视觉协同训练降级、错误归一化丢弃97%动态范围。

Conclusion: 电阻式压力阵列虽然便宜，但通过正确的数据配方（小数据+MAE预训练+标定仿射），可以构建与光学传感器相当的触觉语义理解模型。作者承认失败路径并开放全部权重、代码和记忆层。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [119] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在航空发动机剩余寿命预测中的两种异构性挑战：良性异构（不同运行条件）和对抗异构（投毒攻击），并提出结合个性化学习与鲁棒聚合的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许飞机机队运营商在不共享原始数据的情况下联合训练剩余寿命预测模型，但需应对两类挑战：良性的数据异构性（不同运行条件和故障模式）和恶意的对抗异构性（被入侵的运营商提交投毒更新）。现有方法未系统评估这两种挑战的交互影响，且仅依赖准确率指标无法验证模型安全性。

Method: 使用多任务一维卷积神经网络，在C-MAPSS基准上构建结构非IID数据划分。比较四种良性异构处理方法（共享表示个性化、近端正则化、服务端重加权等），评估五种攻击（包括物理动机的传感器值后门攻击）对四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）的影响。

Result: 共享表示个性化可缩小约70%的本地到集中式RMSE差距（远超近端正则化的21%和服务端重加权的10%）。后门攻击对标准平均聚合达到94.9%的攻击成功率且不影响干净准确率。Krum可将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一能抵御协同攻击者的聚合器。结合个性化与鲁棒聚合可恢复鲁棒性（2.8%攻击成功率），仅需较小准确率代价。

Conclusion: 准确率指标不足以证明模型安全性，必须显式评估攻击成功率。个性化学习单独无法提供防护，需与鲁棒聚合结合以兼顾鲁棒性和性能，在鲁棒更新选择与协作表示学习之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [120] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归残差量化（RRQ）框架，通过从2-bit基模型逐步添加2-bit残差来构建4/6/8-bit表示，实现从单一检查点支持多种位宽精度，无需联合优化且无需校准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法需要为每个目标位宽单独保存检查点，无法灵活适应不同的部署约束（精度、内存、吞吐量权衡），希望实现单检查点多精度支持。

Method: 通过PTQ或RTN获得2-bit基模型，然后逐步添加由RTN生成的2-bit残差来构造4-bit、6-bit和8-bit表示，整个过程免校准、无需联合多位宽优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上构建2/4/6/8-bit全RTN包仅需1293秒，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍；在六个最新LLM上，6和8位精度具有竞争力，4位表现因模型而异。

Conclusion: RRQ是一种高效、灵活的量化框架，以极低构建成本实现单检查点多精度输出，在6/8位下保持竞争精度。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [121] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAMP（Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer），一个面向长时间序列预测的周期感知多尺度模型。CAMP通过自适应周期学习模块为每个输入窗口识别主导频率并生成历史与未来周期分量，通过水平引导的Patch混合器实现位置相关的上下文精炼，并利用多分辨率表示建模去周期后的残差动态。在七个长期预测基准和四个PEMS交通基准上均取得最优或并列最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周期感知预测器通常依赖数据集级别选定的单一周期，无法适应周期性随时间变化或多个周期并存的情况；同时，基于patch的模型对所有patch位置进行统一处理，忽视了不同位置所需的不同信息保留程度；去周期后的残差动态跨越多个时间分辨率，单一尺度难以充分描述。

Method: CAMP包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应周期学习模块，为每个输入窗口独立识别主导频率，无需预定义周期长度，直接生成历史和未来的周期分量；(2) 水平引导的Patch混合器，根据patch位置进行差异化处理——远离预测边界的patch获得更广泛的上下文精炼，靠近预测边界的patch信息被更直接地保留；(3) 多分辨率表示模块，对去周期后的残差进行时间对齐的多尺度建模，捕捉不同尺度上的互补动态。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准中，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中，取得最高的MSE胜出次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过自适应周期识别、位置感知的patch处理和去周期残差的多尺度建模，有效解决了真实时间序列中多变周期、patch位置差异和多分辨率动态等挑战，在长时序预测任务上展现出卓越性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种精确预算的微分层，通过LapSum屏障和归一化二阶矩约束实现从密集等质量到硬top-k的稀疏掩码选择，并给出了饱和比例的总体预测、近二值极限律和最坏情况保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的top-k硬选择会阻断梯度，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码的"硬度"与所选质量耦合在一起，导致难以在保持精确选择预算的同时实现可微的稀疏化。

Method: LaPrune引入LapSum屏障保持选择质量，并通过归一化二阶矩约束在不同预算下将掩码从密集等质量分配推向硬top-k，同时推导了饱和比例的总体预测、近二值极限律和最坏情况下的近零比例保证。归一化硬度参数对分数尺度具有不变性。

Result: LaPrune实现了数学上精确预算的可微稀疏选择，其归一化硬度参数对分数尺度保持不变，而固定LapSum温度则不具备该性质。

Conclusion: LaPrune通过解耦掩码硬度与所选质量的关系，为深度学习中稀疏模型的top-k选择提供了一种兼具可微性、精确预算和尺度不变性的新方法。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [123] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SJEPA，一种无重构的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）框架，通过混合符号-神经网络转移模型学习具有紧凑符号描述的预测表示，核心原则是学习最简单且充分的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合嵌入预测架构虽然能学习抽象状态，但其转移模型通常是不透明的神经网络映射，缺乏可解释性和符号化描述能力，难以理解系统内在动力学规律。

Method: 混合转移模型由符号动力学定律加上带正则化的神经修正项组成。通过表示约束保留信息丰富的非塌缩预测坐标，通过算子压缩偏好低复杂度的符号-神经转移。框架支持交替表示-方程学习和固定表示的符号动力学拟合两种模式。

Result: 在受控摆实验中，联合学习相比事后拟合能发现更简单的符号动力学，具有更低的长期展开误差和散度；无约束的一步诊断验证了预测的表示塌缩捷径。在语法误设下，修正正则化保留了可表示的符号机制，并将神经分量导向残差动力学。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性与符号-神经分配之间存在可控的权衡关系。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [124] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NOVA-KV方法，将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，通过推导闭合形式的最优变换和等体积分组的向量量化器，在两比特每元素下显著恢复长上下文检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码时KV缓存的加载是带宽瓶颈，降低缓存大小可同时提升解码速度和服务器容量。现有方法要么与数据无关，要么未基于失真准则推导变换，且依赖随机或Hadamard旋转与固定宽度标量量化器，在低比特率下性能次优。

Method: 将KV缓存量化形式化为变换编码问题，失真相对于注意力乘积误差定义；从校准统计量推导键和值的闭合形式最优变换（高分辨率模型下）；证明最优键变换非正交但满足广义Parseval关系，使注意力感知失真等价于变换域的MSE；将系数分组为等体积分区使等大小码本达到变速率最优。

Result: 在两比特每元素下，NOVA-KV在可比吞吐量下恢复了标量量化方法丢失的大部分长上下文检索精度。

Conclusion: 通过将KV量化从数据无关的固定变换提升为基于失真准则的变换编码框架，NOVA-KV在固定比特预算下同时实现了更好的精度-吞吐量权衡。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [125] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空图Transformer框架，用于边缘计算场景下的流量预测。该框架通过图神经网络捕捉服务区域间的空间相关性，并利用Transformer的自注意力机制学习长时间范围的时间依赖模式，将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦，在真实蜂窝网络数据集上优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的流量预测对于主动资源管理至关重要，但现有的循环神经网络方法虽然能捕捉短期动态，却难以在非平稳条件下建模长时程的流量演化，缺乏对长期时间依赖和空间相关性的联合建模能力。

Method: 提出一个时空图Transformer框架：使用图神经网络（GNN）捕捉服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer的自注意力机制学习历史流量观测中的长距离时间模式，并将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦以支持大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，该图Transformer在多个预测时域上一致优于基于循环图结构的基线模型（GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU），并且生成的预测结果相比被动资源管理策略能实现更有效的主动资源供应并降低过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强的注意力机制在构建智能自适应的边缘计算系统中具有显著潜力，能够有效应对复杂动态的流量预测需求。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [126] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecDrop，一种无参数、无学习的固定路由方案，用于探究MoE网络中路由性能瓶颈的根源。实验表明，在训练信号粒度与目标类别对齐的视觉任务中，SpecDrop超越参数匹配的基线；但在模糊划分的语言任务中，路由机制几乎与No-Routing对照组无异。结论是：路由是否有效取决于训练信号粒度与目标类别的对齐程度，而非路由算法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE网络通过学习的路由器、门控和负载均衡损失追求专业化，但在参数总量匹配时，学习到的路由器可能表现不如等权重的No-Routing基线。作者希望厘清瓶颈究竟在于路由算法本身，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐程度。

Method: SpecDrop是一种固定且无参数的路由方案：每个分支对分配到的类别获得权重p_a，对其他类别获得较小的泄漏权重p_i，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，不涉及任何学习到的路由参数或辅助损失，推理时需要类别标签。在视觉任务（每张图有单一超类标签）中验证，同时也在语言建模和指令微调等模糊划分任务中验证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100（ResNet-110）上达到79.23%，在ImageNet-1K（ViT-S/16）上达到79.89%，超越参数匹配且不用标签的基线（CIFAR-100比dense高+4.75，ImageNet-1K比No-Routing+SE对照组高+6.53）。但当基线与SpecDrop同样利用标签时，直接掩码稠密模型的输出更强（85.2/83.7）。SpecDrop的贡献在于将标签转化为训练后的模块化结构：58%/100%的分支-类别对齐，掩码增益为0.00（CIFAR）/ +1.06（ImageNet）。在模糊划分（SlimPajama-6B语言建模和SuperNI指令微调）中，路由机制与No-Routing基线在种子噪声范围内无差异。

Conclusion: 训练信号粒度与目标类别的对齐程度决定了路由是否有效，而非路由算法本身。SpecDrop的价值不在于纯准确率上的超越（因为标签感知的密集掩码基线更强），而在于将标签信息内化为模块化结构的能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training. On fuzzy partitions, where training units span multiple categories (SlimPajama-6B language modeling with a 30M Transformer; SuperNI instruction tuning over Llama-3.2-1B with LoRA), the routing mechanism reduces to the matched No-Routing controls within seed noise, the null our thesis predicts. Granularity alignment, not algorithm choice, localizes when routing helps. Code: https://github.com/Beryex/SpecDrop

</details>


### [127] [Out-Of-The-Loop Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04113)
*Gustavo Sutter,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多保真度贝叶斯优化（MF-BO）在真实场景中的一个关键问题：当最优保真度函数过于昂贵无法直接纳入优化循环，但存在历史高保真度数据（如从先前实验中获得的黄金标准数据）时，标准MF-BO算法的表现不佳。作者提出结合历史高保真度数据及任务描述符（可显式给定或从非结构化元数据中提取）来缓解该问题，并在合成函数、化学和超参数优化等真实问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多多保真度贝叶斯优化任务中，真正的最高保真度函数过于昂贵，无法作为优化循环的一部分直接查询。然而，实践者往往拥有来自先前实验的黄金标准数据（最高保真度观测值），这些数据可能为当前任务提供有价值的信息。标准MF-BO算法未利用这类历史数据，导致在上述真实场景中表现次优，即使在理想假设下也是如此。

Method: 作者首先论证了标准MF-BO算法在本文所描述的真实场景中的次优性。随后提出了一种方法，将历史高保真度数据与任务描述符（可以显式给出或从非结构化元数据中提取）整合到MF-BO框架中，以补充当前优化过程中的信息。

Result: 在合成函数、化学领域的实际问题和超参数优化任务上，所提出的方法展现了有效性，验证了整合历史高保真度数据能够改善MF-BO在真实场景中的优化性能。

Conclusion: 标准MF-BO算法在最高保真度函数不可查询但存在历史高保真度数据的真实场景中表现次优；通过结合历史数据与任务描述符可以有效缓解这一问题，提升优化效果。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a ubiquitous problem in science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions with cheaper lower-fidelity proxies available. Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MF-BO) is a principled approach to this problem, leveraging correlations across different fidelities when querying the objective. However, for many important MF-BO tasks, the true highest-fidelity function is prohibitively expensive to be part of the optimization loop. Nevertheless, practitioners often have gold standard data (observations of the highest-fidelity function) obtained from previous experiments that might provide information for the current task. For instance, in molecular optimization, chemists often pick the top-$k$ candidate molecules using various computer simulations, and later reveal their true objective function values. In this work, we demonstrate the suboptimality of standard MF-BO algorithms in the real-world scenarios above, even under ideal assumptions. Next, we mitigate this problem by incorporating historical high-fidelity data accompanied by task descriptors---which can be explicitly given or extracted from unstructured metadata. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on synthetic functions, as well as real-world problems in chemistry and hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [128] [LiNC: Lightweight Noise Correction via Per-Sample Trust and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04147)
*Abhishek Moturu,Babak Taati,Anna Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级噪声校正方法LiNC，通过为每个训练样本添加单个可训练的信任参数，在学习过程中动态决定使用观测标签还是模型自身预测，从而有效应对医学影像数据集中的标签噪声问题。该方法在10个MedMNISTv2数据集、最高50%噪声率下均取得了稳定的准确率提升和良好的错误标签检测效果，且训练开销几乎可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集中普遍存在标签噪声（如评分者间差异、标注错误、模糊病例等），这会严重影响机器学习模型的可靠性和临床有效性，亟需一种高效且轻量的噪声校正方法。

Method: LiNC为每个训练样本引入一个信任参数，训练时使用观测标签与模型预测分布的凸组合作为学习目标。信任参数的梯度方向在训练早期能将干净样本与噪声样本分离，随后通过3分量高斯混合模型将信任值分为干净、模糊和噪声三类，对噪声样本执行软校正阶段和最终硬校正阶段。

Result: 在MedMNISTv2的10个2D数据集上，在最高50%标签噪声条件下，LiNC在准确率和错误标签检测方面均取得了一致的提升，且额外内存随训练集大小线性增长，训练时间复杂度仍由基础网络主导。

Conclusion: LiNC通过极其轻量化的信任参数机制，在几乎不增加计算开销的前提下有效校正了医学影像数据中的标签噪声，兼顾了准确性和可扩展性，具有较高的临床实用价值。

Abstract: Label noise is common in medical imaging datasets due to factors such as inter-rater variability, annotation errors, and ambiguous cases. This can severely undermine the reliability and clinical effectiveness of machine learning models trained using those datasets. To address this challenge, we introduce Lightweight Noise Correction (LiNC), which adds a single trainable trust parameter per training sample and learns when to use the observed label and when to defer to the model during a standard training loop. The key idea is to train using a convex combination of the observed label and the model's own predictive distribution, controlled by a per-sample trust parameter. We show that the gradient of this objective drives trust values in opposite directions for clean versus noisy samples in the early training phase, yielding separable trust distributions. We use a 3-component Gaussian Mixture Model over the trust values to separate them into clean, ambiguous, and noisy cases and then execute a short soft-correction phase on the noisy cases and a final hard correction phase. Experiments on ten 2D datasets from MedMNISTv2 under label noise of up to 50% show consistent gains in accuracy and strong mislabel detection. LiNC adds negligible asymptotic overhead: the training-time complexity remains dominated by the base network, with additional memory growing linearly with the size of the training set.

</details>


### [129] [MINT: Tensor Decomposition on Stacked Recurrence Matrices for Time Series Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04157)
*Kaamil Kaka,Audrey Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Zachary Zimmerman,Vikram Jayaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的时间序列数据挖掘原语——张量化自相似矩阵（tensorized self-similarity matrices），用于处理大规模单变量时间序列数据集（N个长度为n的时间序列，子序列窗口长度为m）。该方法计算大小为 N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1) 的点图（dot plots）张量，并利用张量分解方法挖掘共聚类模式。实验在轨道交通、电力需求、风力涡轮机和汽车交通数据上验证，MINT流水线能有效在含有规律性motif的数据中实现跨传感器模式的共聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归图（Recurrence plots）作为一种时间序列数据挖掘原语已广泛应用于多个领域（如恒星光变曲线、声音波形、CCT遥测），但现有方法在处理大规模多序列数据集时缺乏有效的扩展性和多元数据处理能力，因此需要一种新的、基于张量的原始方法来提升时间序列模式的挖掘效率。

Method: 提出张量化自相似矩阵作为新的时间序列原语，将N条长度为n的时间序列（子序列窗口为m）转换为N×(n-m+1)×(n-m+1)的三阶张量点图，再利用张量分解技术对结果张量进行挖掘，以发现共聚类的跨传感器模式。该张量方法天然可扩展到多变量数据集。

Result: 在轨道交通（MINT）、电力需求、风力涡轮机及汽车交通四类真实数据集上进行验证，结果表明MINT流水线能够有效地在含有规则间隔motif的高规律性数据集中实现跨传感器模式的共聚类。

Conclusion: 张量化自相似矩阵结合张量分解方法为时间序列聚类分析提供了一种新颖且可扩展的原语，特别适用于含有周期性motif的大规模时间序列数据集，未来有望推广至多变量场景及更广泛的应用领域。

Abstract: Recurrence plots are a time series data mining primitive applied to a variety of domains (e.g. star light curves, sound waveforms, CCT telemetry). This work proposes tensorized self-similarity matrices as a primitive for univariate time series datasets ($N\times n$) of $N$ time series of length $n$ with a subsequence window of length $m$, and whose tensor-based nature is naturally extensible to multivariate datasets. The proposed method to compute this primitive computes dot plots of size $N \times (n-m+1) \times (n-m+ 1)$ from these datasets, where the subsequent tensor is mined using tensor decomposition methods to mine for co-clustered patterns. We demonstrate our results in mass rapid transit, electricity demand, wind turbine, and car traffic data, finding the MINT pipeline effectively co-clusters cross-sensor patterns in highly regular datasets containing motifs at regular intervals.

</details>


### [130] [Understanding Fault Tolerance of Adversarially Robust Pruned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04173)
*Manali Dangarikar,Cory Merkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了剪枝、对抗训练和硬件故障注入三者如何共同影响卷积神经网络的鲁棒性。实验表明对抗训练虽提升了对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但增加了对权重卡零故障的敏感性；而剪枝并未显著增加故障敏感性，且剪枝水平的变化对故障率和攻击强度影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络部署在资源受限的神经形态硬件上同时面临三个挑战：剪枝压缩、对抗扰动攻击和硬件权重故障。尽管这些因素已被单独研究，但它们的联合效应对模型可靠性的影响却鲜有关注，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用一个紧凑的三层CNN在MNIST数据集上进行三项实验：(1)比较自然训练和对抗训练模型在同时遭受硬件故障和对抗攻击时的容错能力；(2)评估剪枝对对抗鲁棒性的影响；(3)刻画故障率、对抗扰动幅度和剪枝水平三者联合的准确率表面。

Result: 对抗训练提高了对输入扰动的鲁棒性，但增加了对卡零权重故障的敏感性；与直觉相反，剪枝并未显著增加故障敏感性，且不同剪枝水平对故障率和攻击强度的影响很小。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在设计部署于神经形态硬件的DNN时，需要联合考虑对抗鲁棒性和硬件可靠性，而不是孤立地处理每个因素。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) deployed on resource-constrained neuromorphic hardware face three concurrent challenges: the need for model compression through pruning, vulnerability to adversarial input perturbations, and susceptibility to hardware-induced weight faults such as stuck-at-zero errors. While each of these factors has been studied in isolation, their combined effects on model reliability have received little attention. This paper presents an empirical investigation of how pruning, adversarial training, and hardware fault injection interact to affect the robustness of convolutional neural networks. Using a compact three-layer CNN trained on MNIST, we conduct three experiments: (1) comparing the fault tolerance of naturally and adversarially trained models under simultaneous hardware faults and adversarial attacks, (2) evaluating how pruning affects adversarial robustness, and (3) characterizing the joint accuracy surface across fault rates, adversarial perturbation magnitudes, and pruning levels. Our results show that adversarial training improves robustness against input perturbations but increases sensitivity to stuck-at-zero weight faults. Contrary to intuition, pruning did not significantly increase fault sensitivity, and varying the pruning level had little effect across fault rates and attack strengths. These results highlight the need to jointly consider adversarial robustness and hardware reliability.

</details>


### [131] [TS2TabPFN: Time Series Classification and Extrinsic Regression through Feature Extraction and a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04174)
*Gabriel da Costa Merlin,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS2TabPFN框架，将显式特征提取与TabPFN 2.5表格基础模型相结合，用于时间序列分类（TSC）和外在回归（TSER）任务，实验表明其在TSER任务上显著超越现有最优模型，在TSC任务上也能超越大多数当前最佳算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型往往分别侧重于特征工程或端到端深度学习，特征提取的质量与复杂架构的自动预测能力之间存在割裂，需要一种能够同时兼顾两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出TS2TabPFN框架，将显式特征提取模块与TabPFN 2.5（一种面向表格数据的基础模型）进行集成，利用基础模型的预测能力来处理时间序列数据。

Result: TS2TabPFN在TSER任务上以统计显著性大幅优于现有最优模型，在TSC任务上也能超越大部分当前最优算法，表现出了鲁棒性和高效性。

Conclusion: 将基础模型与结构化特征相结合能够克服单一范式的局限性，为时间序列任务确立了新的最优水平。

Abstract: Time series data are ubiquitous in practical applications, where classification (TSC) and extrinsic regression (TSER) have emerged as essential tasks for obtaining value from temporal sequences. While the literature has seen significant progress through feature-based and deep learning models, existing methods often focus either on the quality of feature extraction or on the intrinsic predictive power of complex architectures applied to raw data. This division creates a gap between the control offered by feature engineering and the automated performance of end-to-end models. This paper proposes TS2TabPFN, a framework that bridges this gap by integrating explicit feature extraction with TabPFN 2.5, a cutting-edge foundation model for tabular data, to leverage its predictive capabilities. Our extensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that TS2TabPFN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art models in TSER tasks with statistical significance, providing a robust and efficient alternative for TSC and surpassing most of the currently best-performing algorithms. These results suggest that combining foundation models with structured features overcomes single-paradigm limitations, establishing a new time series state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [132] [A Comparative Study of Feature Selection Methods for EHR Diagnosis Codes in Opioid Use Disorder Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04180)
*Zihan Ding,Yinan Liu,Tengfei Ma,Rachel Wong,George Leibowitz,Benjamin Littenberg,Xia Zheng,Richard N. Rosenthal,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了五种用于阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）预测的诊断相关特征选择方法，发现NTK敏感性方法在准确性和稳定性上表现最佳，LLM引导的特征选择虽独立性能较低但能提供互补的临床有意义的信号，且特征数量增加会带来性能提升但存在边际递减效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）预测建模中特征选择至关重要，因为输入变量通常存在高维、稀疏、噪声和冗余的问题。大规模特征集会增加计算负担和过拟合风险，同时降低模型的可解释性，限制了在临床环境中的实用价值。因此需要系统比较不同的特征选择范式以找到最优方案。

Method: 研究聚焦于诊断相关特征，在统一的预处理和评估框架下比较了五种特征选择范式：复发富集（recurrence enrichment）、NTK驱动的早期梯度敏感性（NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity）、LightGBM-SHAP、Elastic Net以及大语言模型（LLM）引导的语义选择。每种方法均通过下游预测性能、重采样稳定性和罕见诊断代码的表示程度进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，性能随特征预算增大而提升，但超过中等规模后收益递减。NTK敏感性方法在准确性和稳定性之间提供了最佳的整体平衡；LLM引导的选择尽管独立性能较低，但能贡献互补的临床有意义的信号。

Conclusion: 该研究为EHR场景下的特征选择提供了系统性的比较基准，NTK敏感性方法可作为可靠的默认选择，而LLM引导的特征选择可作为补充手段增强临床语义信号，两者结合可能获得更优的综合效果。

Abstract: Feature selection is a critical step in electronic health record (EHR)-based predictive modeling, where input variables are often high-dimensional, sparse, noisy, and redundant. Large feature sets not only increase computational burden and overfitting risk, but also make model interpretation difficult, leading to limited usefulness in clinical settings. In this study, we focus on diagnosis-related features and compare five feature selection paradigms for opioid use disorder (OUD) prediction: recurrence enrichment, NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity, LightGBM-SHAP, Elastic Net, and large language model (LLM)-guided semantic selection. We use a unified preprocessing and evaluation framework and assess each method by downstream predictive performance, resampling stability, and representation of infrequent diagnosis codes. Our results demonstrate that performance improves with larger feature budgets with diminishing returns beyond a moderate size. NTK sensitivity provides the best overall balance of accuracy and stability, and LLM-guided selection contributes complementary clinically meaningful signals despite lower standalone performance.

</details>


### [133] [Unscented KalmanNet: a hybrid deep learning filter with calibrated posterior covariance for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04201)
*Minhyeok Ko,Abdollah Shafieezadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unscented KalmanNet（UKN），一种混合递归估计器，在UKF框架上增加两个学习的组件（NoiseNet和GainNet），分别预测时变噪声协方差和对滤波增益进行残差校正，通过校准感知的训练目标联合优化精度与协方差校准，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UKF在非线性系统状态估计中广泛使用，但实际应用中未知的时变噪声统计量和模型失配会同时降低估计精度和协方差校准质量。现有的学习型滤波器大多基于扩展卡尔曼滤波器构建，要么放弃显式协方差输出，要么学习不确定性但无法纠正由模型失配导致的增益偏差。因此需要一种既保持UKF sigma点协方差递推结构，又能通过学习组件同时改善精度和校准的方法。

Method: UKN在UKF框架上增加两个结构上不同的学习组件：NoiseNet将时变过程噪声和测量噪声协方差预测为对固定基线的有界乘性修正，保证正定性；GainNet对解析卡尔曼增益施加有界残差修正。训练目标采用校准感知策略，将状态误差与协方差一致性、创新一致性三项通过自适应权重联合优化。

Result: UKN在三个合成系统和UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上与UKF、KalmanNet和Bayesian KalmanNet对比，在全部四个示例中取得最低的聚合状态估计误差。合成案例中相对UKF的RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%。11次飞行的留一序列交叉验证显示平均位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%。UKN还获得最低的折间变异性，归一化NEES和经验覆盖率最接近名义值。

Conclusion: UKN通过将学习组件嵌入UKF框架，在保持显式sigma点协方差递推的同时，有效提升了非线性状态估计的精度和协方差校准质量，在合成数据和真实飞行数据上均展现出一致且稳健的性能优势，验证了混合学习-模型驱动滤波器设计的有效性。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems is commonly performed with the Unscented Kalman filter (UKF), which propagates the state moments through deterministic sigma points and reports a posterior covariance at every step. In practice, however, unknown and time-varying noise statistics and model mismatch degrade both estimation accuracy and covariance calibration. Existing learned filters improve accuracy but are largely built on the extended Kalman filter and either forgo an explicit covariance or learn uncertainty without correcting mismatch-induced gain bias. This paper introduces the Unscented KalmanNet (UKN), a hybrid recursive estimator that augments the UKF with two structurally distinct learned components while preserving its explicit sigma-point covariance recursion. NoiseNet predicts time-varying process and measurement covariances as bounded multiplicative corrections to fixed baselines, guaranteeing positive definiteness, while GainNet applies a bounded residual correction to the analytical gain. A calibration-aware training objective combines state error with covariance- and innovation-consistency terms through adaptive weights, jointly optimizing accuracy and calibration. UKN is benchmarked against UKF, KalmanNet, and Bayesian KalmanNet on three synthetic systems and UZH-FPV real-flight data. It achieves the lowest aggregate state-estimation error in all four examples and reduces RMSE by $26.4$-$49.7\%$ compared to UKF in the synthetic cases. Leave-one-sequence-out cross-validation over 11 flights shows $22.4\%$ and $34.3\%$ reductions in mean position and velocity RMSE, respectively. UKN also yields the lowest fold-to-fold variability, with normalized NEES and empirical coverage closest to nominal values among the other filters.

</details>


### [134] [From Non-Convex Self-Concordant Regularization to Scalable Quasi-Newton Training of PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04206)
*Chenhao Si,Kang An,Shiqian Ma,Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE，一种面向物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练的自一致启发式拟牛顿方法，通过递减耦合的移位割线几何实现高精度求解偏微分方程，在多个经典方程上优于BFGS和自缩放Broyden基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练通常需要高精度的拟牛顿精细优化，但其残差目标可能存在不定、近似奇异且尺度不良的局部曲率，传统拟牛顿方法难以稳定处理这些挑战。

Method: SCORE方法的核心机制是利用从学习逆度量计算的单一拟牛顿递减量，同时决定强Wolfe检验的候选步长和用于定义下一个割线几何的自适应移位。移位位移表示沿接受步长的平均移位度量作用，无需构建Hessian或Hessian-向量乘积。通过强Wolfe接受、回退线搜索和标准曲率保护实现全局化。

Result: 在粘性Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、Korteweg-de Vries和复Ginzburg-Landau方程上的实验表明，SCORE比BFGS和自缩放Broyden基线获得更低的最终误差。Burgers消融实验显示移位曲率稳定化和递减基步长选择对高精度细化有互补贡献。

Conclusion: SCORE通过将拟牛顿递减量与自适应移位相结合，在无需Hessian构建的前提下有效稳定了PINN训练中的割线模型，为高精度偏微分方程求解提供了一种新的优化框架。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often require high-accuracy quasi-Newton refinement to obtain reliable partial differential equation solutions, but their residual objectives can exhibit indefinite, nearly singular, and poorly scaled local curvature. Regularized quasi-Newton methods provide established mechanisms for stabilizing secant models, while self-concordant methods provide local-metric rules for curvature-dependent step selection. Building on these two lines of work, we propose SCORE, a self-concordance-inspired quasi-Newton method with decrement-coupled shifted secant geometry for PINN training. Its distinguishing mechanism is that a single quasi-Newton decrement computed from the learned inverse metric jointly determines a strong-Wolfe-tested candidate step and an adaptive shift used to define the next secant geometry. The shifted displacement represents the action of an averaged shifted metric along the accepted step, while requiring neither Hessian construction nor Hessian-vector products. Under a local spectral-equivalence condition, we show that the quasi-Newton decrement and candidate step remain comparable to their counterparts in a positive shifted metric, and recover the normalized self-concordant rule in the matched-metric case. Strong Wolfe acceptance, fallback line search, and standard curvature safeguards provide globalization without modifying the underlying PINN objective. Experiments on the viscous Burgers, Kuramoto--Sivashinsky, Korteweg--de Vries, and complex Ginzburg--Landau equations show that SCORE attains lower final errors than the tested BFGS and self-scaled Broyden baselines. The Burgers ablation further indicates that shifted curvature stabilization and decrement-based step selection make complementary contributions to high-accuracy refinement.

</details>


### [135] [Attention-Only White-Box Transformer via LeJEPA-Based Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04213)
*Yang Bai,Linyuan Wang,Haoyang Jiang,Nuolin Sun,Libin Hou,Bin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从联合视角重新审视白盒网络推导与自监督学习范式，提出利用LeJEPA自监督框架优化稀疏率缩减目标中的扩展项，并通过ADMM推导出仅含注意力机制的白盒Transformer（无需ISTA结构或MLP层）。在CIFAR-10（88.88%）和CIFAR-100（63.54%）上取得与原始CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）相当的性能，同时参数减少约31%。此外还发现标准ViT中用ReLU替换MLP块可在知识蒸馏下减少66%参数且保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将白盒网络的推导（通过优化算法）和自监督学习范式解耦处理。作者发现LeJEPA自监督框架假设各向同性高斯分布为最优嵌入分布，这与稀疏率缩减目标中的扩展项R(Z)在概念上等价，因此提出将两者联合考虑，以简化白盒Transformer结构并减少参数冗余。

Method: 1) 利用LeJEPA自监督范式直接优化稀疏率缩减目标中的扩展项R(Z)；2) 通过交替方向乘子法（ADMM）推导其余项R^c(Z|U_[K])+λ||Z||_0，得到仅含注意力机制的白盒Transformer，移除了原始的ISTA结构和MLP层；3) 进一步在标准ViT上探索用ReLU激活替换所有MLP块在知识蒸馏下的效果。

Result: 1) 注意仅白盒Transformer在Base规模下达到CIFAR-10准确率88.88%、CIFAR-100准确率63.54%，与原始CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）相当，但参数量减少约31%；2) 在标准ViT上，用ReLU替换所有MLP块并在知识蒸馏下训练，可移除约66%的参数且保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 这项工作验证了白盒网络优化与自监督学习可以联合设计，通过等效性观察简化了白盒Transformer的结构（仅保留注意力机制），在保持性能的同时大幅减少参数。同时，对标准ViT中MLP模块冗余性的初步探索也为未来高效Transformer架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Existing studies on self-supervised learning for white-box networks typically decouple the derivation of white-box networks via optimization algorithms from self-supervised learning paradigms. In this work, we instead revisit the two components from a joint perspective. The LeJEPA-based self-supervised framework assumes an isotropic Gaussian distribution as the optimal embedding distribution for downstream tasks, which is conceptually equivalent to the expansion term $R(Z)$ in the sparse rate reduction objective guiding white-box Transformer optimization. Building on this observation, we use the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm to optimize $R(Z)$, and derive the remaining terms $R^{c}(Z\mid U_{[K]})+λ\lVert Z\rVert_{0}$ via the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) into an attention-only Transformer that dispenses with the ISTA structure or MLP layers of the original design. Experimental results demonstrate that our attention-only white-box Transformer achieves classification accuracies of $88.88\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.54\%$ on CIFAR-100 at the Base scale under the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm, while the original white-box Transformer CRATE achieves classification accuracies of $89.18\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.56\%$ on CIFAR-100. Our model achieves competitive performance while reducing the parameter count by roughly $31\%$. Beyond the white-box setting, we further investigate standard ViTs and find that replacing all MLP blocks with ReLU activations under knowledge distillation removes approximately 66\% of the parameters while preserving competitive accuracy, motivating further investigation into the potential redundancy of MLP modules in standard ViT architectures.

</details>


### [136] [Random features for Grassmannian kernel approximation with bounded rank-one projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04227)
*Rémi Delogne,Laurent Jacques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族基于秩一投影的随机特征映射方法，用于在Grassmannian流形（低维子空间）上进行可扩展的核机学习，通过周期或二值非线性变换逼近旋转不变的Grassmannian核，大幅降低计算和存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
